- **From H to I**: H -> I (1 edge)
- **From I to Q**: I -> Q (1 edge)

  - **From F to E**: F -> E (1 edge)
  - **From E to S**: E -> S (1 edge)
  - **From S to C**: S -> C (1 edge)
  - **From C to N**: C -> N (1 edge)
  - **From N to R**: N -> R (1 edge)
  - **From R to J**: R -> J (1 edge)
  - **From J to I**: J -> I (1 edge)
  - **From I to Q**: I -> Q (1 edge)

This path is much longer and not optimal.

After evaluating possible paths, the shortest path is indeed B -> G -> H -> I -> Q.
